# Physics: question papers in full

111 unique papers · 2022-23 to 2025-26 · Data Science, Information Technology, Common (all branches), Artificial Intelligence, Computer Engineering, Electronics and Telecommunication, AI and Data Science, Computer Science and Business Systems, Cyber Security, Mechatronics, Civil, AI and Machine Learning, Computer Science, Mechanical

Each paper: year, exam, semester, departments and programmes, then its full text as extracted. Scanned papers were read by OCR, so equations and symbols may contain recognition errors. Structured data: [`questions.jsonl`](questions.jsonl) (one record per question) and [`papers.jsonl`](papers.jsonl) (one record per paper). Index of files: [`README.md`](README.md).

b. A wedge air film is enclosed between two glass plates touching at one edge and   6
–3
separated by a wire of 0.06 x 10 m diameter at a distance of 0.15 m from the edge.
–7
Calculate the fringe width. The light of wavelength 6 x 10 m from the broad source is
allowed to fall normally on the film.
c. Explain the origin of nuclear force and state its characteristics.   4
6.   20
a. What is an optical fiber? Explain the following terms related to optical fiber: a) critical   10
angle, b) acceptance cone, c) numerical aperture and d) Normalized frequency or V-
number.
b. How many orders will be visible if the wavelength of the incident radiation is 5000 Å   6
and the number of lines on the grating is 2620 in one inch?
c. A UHF television loop antenna has a diameter of 11 cm. The magnetic field of a TV   4
signal is normal to the plane of the loop, and, at one instant of time, its magnitude
changes at the rate of 0.16 T/s. The magnetic field is uniform. What emf is induced in
the antenna?
